 `app.online`/`useOnline()` (AppProvider): server query/observe, ephemeral mutate, act; no bearer/lifecycle; durable writes: app.mutate. `guide social`/`guide ephemeral`: snapshots/typing/quotes/bounded screens.
 
 ## Generic read props and outcomes
 
-An empty complete list is `absent` only with `cut:null`, no continuation or blocking prediction. List props: `Read<T[]>` (`T extends { id: string }`); operation props: `UseQuery<typeof api.op>`.
-Narrow `write.state === "sent"` before `result` (omitted on replay); failed carries `why`; pending is not success.
-`useQuery` returns a live Read, without `refetch()`. For a one-shot refetch, `await client.query(...)` after `sent`.
+An empty complete list is `absent` only with `cut:null`, no continuation or blocking prediction. List props: `Read<T[]>` (`T extends { id: string }`); operation props: `UseQuery<typeof api.op>`. Ordinary `run()` waits for `sent | failed`, independent of query subscribers or component unmount; keep the App alive to collect its receipt. Offline it waits for reconnect. Explicit `{ waitSeconds: 0 }` returns custody immediately; pending is not success.
+`sentResult(write)` returns the fresh result (including null) or undefined for pending, failed and replayed receipts; failed carries why.
 
-## Row queries
-Share/paginate (`usePage` queries take `c: cursor ?` with `after c`); authorization remains dependent.
-Eight active; eight queued observation attempts start/turn; direct queries and timed presence refreshes use separate paths. Local budgets pause while queued, never replenish. Online evidence overtakes ≤8 times; occupied slots can withdraw.
+```tsx
+import { matchRead, otherwiseAll, sentResult, useMutation, useQuery } from "snapback4/react";
+const read = useQuery(api.staffNotes, {}, { skip: !granted }); // avoids unauthorized requests until role grant
+const text = matchRead(read, { ...otherwiseAll(() => "Unavailable"), absent: () => "No notes", present: rows => `${rows.length} notes` });
+const add = useMutation(api.add);
+const write = await add.run({ title: "Proof" });
+const result = sentResult(write);
+if (result !== undefined) console.log(result); // null is a legitimate fresh result
+if (write.state === "failed") console.log(write.why.message);
+```
 
-Server/composed scopes preserve unrelated rooms; unknown selectors withdraw table-wide.
-Flashes: `inspect(client)`, `useInspection(import.meta.env.DEV)`: [pressure](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#inspect-subscription-pressure).
+A complete empty list is absent, not loading; handle it alongside rows:
+
+```tsx
+const rows = matchRead(read, { loading: () => "Loading…", denied: why => why.message,
+  absent: () => "No notes", partial: () => "Partial results", present: data => `${data.length} notes` });
+```
+
+Keyed counts: hidden key → null; authorized missing count group → 0; public count → number. Choose `count ?? 0` explicitly. `useQuery` is live; after sent, `client.query(...)` reads once.
+
+## Row queries
+Share/paginate (`usePage` queries take `c: cursor ?` with `after c`); authorization remains dependent. Eight active; eight queued observation attempts; direct queries and timed presence refreshes use separate paths. `inspect(client)` and `useInspection(import.meta.env.DEV)` report pressure ([reference](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#inspect-subscription-pressure)).
 
 ## Expo and acquisition
-Browser: `createApp`; no sync → journal (personas too). Synced: `webDevice()`/writes: "online". Opening refusal preserves replay. Native: `nativeDevice`; Expo Go online; `guide sync`.
-Every milestone: `npm run export:ios`; proof in the background: `witness status --wait`. Rebuild: `npm run native:smoke`. Acquisition/offline reopen/reconnect/write replay: `guide witness`.

Every milestone: `npm run export:ios`; proof in the background: `witness status --wait`. Rebuild: `npm run native:smoke`. Acquisition/offline reopen/reconnect/write replay: `guide witness`.
 
 `readOnly: true` (`createClient`/online `createApp`, `guide read-only`): inactive `useSeen`, no SDK rotation. `refreshSession()`: `E_READ_ONLY_REFRESH`; `expiresAt`: `E_AUTH`.
 
 ## Receipts
 `guide receipts`: `useSeen(api.receiptFamilies.chat, { scope, through, active })` owns retries. SDK owns delivery reporting; screen owns visible `seen` reporting. Supply visible confirmed positions; separate retained history/live decorations.

+
+## Browser proof
+Never wait for `networkidle`: live queries hold a long poll. Use `domcontentloaded`, labelled controls, and this write's fresh `sent` receipt before rows; existing text proves nothing. Visible-form witness and RN-web notes: `guide testing`.

+## Period boundaries and anchors
+
+`periodEnd` is the scheduled firing boundary, not the invocation's finish time.
+`periodStart` is the preceding boundary; the represented period is [periodStart, periodEnd).
+Fixed `every N` schedules anchor to the Unix epoch in UTC, shifted by `at HH:MM`.
+`every 7d` therefore fires on Thursday at 00:00 UTC (1970-01-01 was Thursday),
+not seven days after deploy. `every 7d at 08:00` stays Thursday 08:00 UTC.
+For another weekday, schedule daily and gate the job using day(periodEnd) % 7
+(Unix Thursday is 0, Monday is 4); never assume a deployment-time anchor.
+Calendar month/year schedules use UTC month boundaries; `guide time` has their syntax.
+
 ## Operations
 
 In tests, use `snapback4 clock advance <dur>` or `POST /dev/clock/advance`; each example line advances the running dev once.

-Columns and arguments are required unless their type ends in `?`; omitted optional values are null.
-A table name means a reference: any id string. Its target is never read, and
-membership cannot prove it exists: use `require exists t[id] else CODE`, or
-bind with `r = t[id] ! else CODE`.
-`default` fills omissions; explicit values win.
-There are no floats: `money` and `decimal S` are integers in minor units, and
-a fraction anywhere in JSON, `json` values included, refuses E_INPUT (guide wire).
-Enums widen within text bounds.
-`require exists t[k]`, `require t[k] != null`, and present `if t[k] != null`
-branches narrow identical admitted unique-key reads, including helper arguments.
-Guard nullable keys first (guide indexes). Writes, rebindings and viewer changes invalidate it.
+Required columns/arguments become nullable with `?`; omission becomes null. Table types are references; neither typing nor membership reads the target: use `require exists t[id] else CODE` or `r = t[id] ! else CODE`.
+Defaults fill omissions; explicit values win. Formats: handle and url; email is application validation or identity signup/login, not `format email`.
+Money/decimal are scaled integers; JSON fractions refuse E_INPUT. Identical admitted unique-key reads narrow after exists/non-null guards, including pure helper arguments; writes, rebinding and viewer changes invalidate proof.

For generated queries returning source rows, it keeps one newest observed image per row ID; projections keep list semantics even when they contain a field named `id`. Both read hooks keep the same-key answer marked `revalidating` through refetch, reconnect, slow reads, offline transport and retry exhaustion, with no timeout. Local commits replace it directly after the replica re-read. Answers stay visible and `revalidating` until replaced unless change evidence may revoke a row; then the existing one-second withdrawal bound applies. Inserts and updates to non-policy columns retain the answer through slow or failed retries. Deletes, changes to authorization inputs, and unknown or older evidence retain the bound. This classification is automatic across local commits, online mutation acknowledgements, `/changes` and `/sync`. Successful immediate replica replacements never show `loading`. An older replica read cannot clear a pending notification; sync must apply its cut or a current server answer must replace the display. Offline ephemeral answers keep their original row expiry. Applied local membership removal withdraws the affected audience groups immediately, even before a staged page publishes. Older replica rows cannot return before publication. Local budgets exclude capacity queue wait; online revocation evidence includes it. `keep: false` withdraws immediately; `keep: true` adds a 30-second linger after the last subscriber. Key changes, deadlines, sign-out, credential, store and schema resets still withdraw. A reported program-generation change withdraws immediately, even with no changed tables; the old replica stays unavailable until adoption completes. Offline retention uses `fresh: false` and `since`; no profile identity latch is needed. `useMutation` tracks writes; `useAsset` resolves
 a placed asset. Upload bytes with `client.upload` before passing the asset id to its placement mutation. Operations in `schema.q` are

Effects run in declared Node 24 entries `snapback/workers/<service>.mjs` (`node` on PATH or `SNAPBACK_NODE`; Bun is not accepted, and dev exits at startup without it): dev and serve start, restart and stop them, with service tokens injected and logs redacted. `guide effects` shows `ledger: true` and the stable `meta.idempotencyKey`; a crash after the provider call but before the ledger commit still needs provider idempotency.
 
 Public feeds share an activity union: Following delivers it to each reader; Trending ranks a finite time window (`npx snapback4 guide feeds`). Use the generated [`useFollowing` reader](./CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#following-history) for exact online history, preparation and live prepend; offline it keeps the inbox and reports that older posts are not on this device.

 Membership: no horizon, the conversation's own group and messages' audience prove presence/absence unless a pending prediction blocks absence. `loading` means acquisition or later withdrawal. Never add `with own keys` to admin-written memberships.
 `denied` always blocks. `existence(member) === "absent"` blocks only when fresh and non-revalidating. Stale absence queues; `loading`/`partial` never disable.
 
-`waitSeconds: 0` returns at custody; `inFlight` covers admission, ungated by acquisition after session readiness. Identity I/O: 15s excluding prior admissions; `usePendingWrites()` says “not yet admitted”. Reload loses it. Resumes FIFO; `E_ADMISSION_STORE`/`E_ADMISSION_TIMEOUT` permit retry after repair without remounting.
-Predictions: “Sending…”.

 `usePendingItems({writes,rows,scope,rowKey,intentKey})`: mapped row/intent union through observed settlement. `usePendingState(op,{scope,current,read?,value})`: latest admitted intent. [Reference](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md); non-JS hosts: “The device reply”.
 
 ## Read boundaries
-`query(op,args)` settles ready/denied within 30 seconds including queues/approval. Admitted dependent writes: observed/failed or in complete device predictions. Unrelated writes pass; unknown intersects. Custody awaits events; loading/races before/after waits: four rounds; exhaustion refuses retryable `E_QUERY_RETRY`; permanently unavailable held reads refuse non-retryable `E_QUERY_HELD`. Offline: permitted predictions/data or immediate `E_OFFLINE`, including blockers. Refusals win; partial/stale permitted.
+`query(op,args)` settles ready/denied within 30 seconds including queued work. Dependent writes settle or have complete device predictions; unrelated writes pass. Offline data/predictions can answer; unavailable reads refuse E_OFFLINE. After four rounds, exhaustion refuses retryable `E_QUERY_RETRY`; offline unavailable reads give immediate `E_OFFLINE`; unavailable held reads refuse E_QUERY_HELD. Partial/stale answers remain explicit.

 ## React hooks
-`matchRead(read, …)` (no type arguments; or a page) requires five states: `present(data, read)` excludes null; `partial(data, read)` keeps T; `denied(why, read)`; `absent(read)`/`loading(read)`. `otherwise(read)` covers omitted loading/absent/present; denied/partial stay explicit, or `...otherwiseAll(read => …)` anywhere takes all unnamed (named handlers win). `existence(read)` classifies; `isReady(read)` enables `read.existence`; `void client.close()` in cleanup.
-Types/hooks: `RowOf`/`ResultOf`/`ArgsOf`/`UseQuery`/`UsePage`/`useSignedInViewer()`/`usePersonaApp`; `{ skip: !id }` waits for arguments; `[add, remove].find(isFailed)?.last.why`; helpers take `Outcome | undefined` or `Read<unknown>` ([form](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#status-components-and-a-form)).
-
-| Meaning | UI decision |
-| --- | --- |
-| `loading` | Withheld: keep composers/headers/routes mounted. |
-| `denied` | Show refusal; disable durable action. |
-| `present` | Render values; `incomplete` controls “Partial results”. |
-| `absent` | Proven empty: resolve missing route/row. |
-| `partial` | Uncertain empty, never absence or denial; `incomplete` controls “Partial results”. |
-
-`null`/`[]` is empty; `false`/`0`/nested nulls are present. Empty is `absent` only with `complete`, `cut === null`, no `next`, and no admission or intersecting prediction. Unknown prediction footprints block every read; complete local execution narrows dependencies. `fresh` never demotes; offline `absent` is absence at the acquired watermark. Hidden/missing rows are indistinguishable.
-Route on `present`/`absent`; follow `useAuth().state` (personas: `guide personas`). Reset drafts on account/backend change. Retention grants no authority.
-Deletes, policy changes and unknown/older evidence start a nonrenewable one-second bound; inserts, non-policy updates and plain revalidation retain through failures. Local bounds exclude queueing; online bounds include it. Bounded failure/timeout withdraws. Scope loss withdraws dependent groups; older replicas await publication. `keep: true`: 30-second subscriber-free linger; `keep: false`: immediate withdrawal. Keys, deadlines, sign-out, credentials/store/schema/generation changes withdraw immediately.
-Search: render `page.items`; `readPresentation(page.read)` supplies `pending` (loading), `incomplete` (partial/coverage/cut/continuation), `stale`, `revalidating`, `why?`, `since?`; never permission. Follow `hasMore`/`loadMore`. Unknown counts never hide conversations. [Expo screen](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#a-stable-conversation-screen): backend/account/resource shell preserves drafts.

Types/hooks: `RowOf`/`ResultOf`/`ArgsOf`/`UseQuery`/`UsePage`/`useSignedInViewer()`/`usePersonaApp`; `{ skip: !id }` waits for arguments; `[add, remove].find(isFailed)?.last.why`; helpers take `Outcome | undefined` or `Read<unknown>` ([form](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#status-components-and-a-form)).
+

 ## Generic read props and outcomes
 
-An empty complete list is `absent` only with `cut:null`, no continuation or blocking prediction. List props: `Read<T[]>` (`T extends { id: string }`); operation props: `UseQuery<typeof api.op>`.
-Narrow `write.state === "sent"` before `result` (omitted on replay); failed carries `why`; pending is not success.
-`useQuery` returns a live Read, without `refetch()`. For a one-shot refetch, `await client.query(...)` after `sent`.
+An empty complete list is `absent` only with `cut:null`, no continuation or blocking prediction. List props: `Read<T[]>` (`T extends { id: string }`); operation props: `UseQuery<typeof api.op>`. Ordinary `run()` waits for `sent | failed`, independent of query subscribers or component unmount; keep the App alive to collect its receipt. Offline it waits for reconnect. Explicit `{ waitSeconds: 0 }` returns custody immediately; pending is not success.
+`sentResult(write)` returns the fresh result (including null) or undefined for pending, failed and replayed receipts; failed carries why.
 
-## Row queries
-Share/paginate (`usePage` queries take `c: cursor ?` with `after c`); authorization remains dependent.
-Eight active; eight queued observation attempts start/turn; direct queries and timed presence refreshes use separate paths. Local budgets pause while queued, never replenish. Online evidence overtakes ≤8 times; occupied slots can withdraw.
+```tsx
+import { matchRead, otherwiseAll, sentResult, useMutation, useQuery } from "snapback4/react";
+const read = useQuery(api.staffNotes, {}, { skip: !granted }); // avoids unauthorized requests until role grant
+const text = matchRead(read, { ...otherwiseAll(() => "Unavailable"), absent: () => "No notes", present: rows => `${rows.length} notes` });
+const add = useMutation(api.add);
+const write = await add.run({ title: "Proof" });
+const result = sentResult(write);
+if (result !== undefined) console.log(result); // null is a legitimate fresh result
+if (write.state === "failed") console.log(write.why.message);
+```
 
-Server/composed scopes preserve unrelated rooms; unknown selectors withdraw table-wide.
-Flashes: `inspect(client)`, `useInspection(import.meta.env.DEV)`: [pressure](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#inspect-subscription-pressure).
+A complete empty list is absent, not loading; handle it alongside rows:
+
+```tsx
+const rows = matchRead(read, { loading: () => "Loading…", denied: why => why.message,
+  absent: () => "No notes", partial: () => "Partial results", present: data => `${data.length} notes` });
+```
+
+Keyed counts: hidden key → null; authorized missing count group → 0; public count → number. Choose `count ?? 0` explicitly. `useQuery` is live; after sent, `client.query(...)` reads once.
+
+## Row queries
+Share/paginate (`usePage` queries take `c: cursor ?` with `after c`); authorization remains dependent. Eight active; eight queued observation attempts; direct queries and timed presence refreshes use separate paths. `inspect(client)` and `useInspection(import.meta.env.DEV)` report pressure ([reference](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#inspect-subscription-pressure)).
 
 ## Expo and acquisition
-Browser: `createApp`; no sync → journal (personas too). Synced: `webDevice()`/writes: "online". Opening refusal preserves replay. Native: `nativeDevice`; Expo Go online; `guide sync`.
-Every milestone: `npm run export:ios`; proof in the background: `witness status --wait`. Rebuild: `npm run native:smoke`. Acquisition/offline reopen/reconnect/write replay: `guide witness`.
+Browser: `createApp`; no sync → journal (personas too). Synced: `webDevice()`/writes: "online". Opening refusal preserves replay. Native: `nativeDevice`; Expo Go online; `guide sync`. Every milestone: `npm run export:ios`; proof in the background: `witness status --wait`. Rebuild: `npm run native:smoke`. Acquisition/offline reopen/reconnect/write replay: `guide witness`.
 
 `readOnly: true` (`createClient`/online `createApp`, `guide read-only`): inactive `useSeen`, no SDK rotation. `refreshSession()`: `E_READ_ONLY_REFRESH`; `expiresAt`: `E_AUTH`.
 
 ## Receipts
 `guide receipts`: `useSeen(api.receiptFamilies.chat, { scope, through, active })` owns retries. SDK owns delivery reporting; screen owns visible `seen` reporting. Supply visible confirmed positions; separate retained history/live decorations.
 
-## One-view photos
-[One-view photo recipe](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md): mount above the list; retain handles across refetch; account/unmount revokes late responses; absence/denial/deadline closes.
-`useAsset` revokes on unmount; once media cannot refetch (`E_ASSET_VIEWED`).
-Offline media: [OFFLINE-UPLOADS.md](OFFLINE-UPLOADS.md).
+Once-media handles revoke on unmount ([recipe](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md)); offline media: [OFFLINE-UPLOADS.md](OFFLINE-UPLOADS.md).
+
+## Browser proof
+Never wait for `networkidle`: live queries hold a long poll. Use `domcontentloaded`, labelled controls, and this write's fresh `sent` receipt before rows; existing text proves nothing. Visible-form witness and RN-web notes: `guide testing`.

 still refuses oversized work.
 
+## Period boundaries and anchors
+
+`periodEnd` is the scheduled firing boundary, not the invocation's finish time.
+`periodStart` is the preceding boundary; the represented period is [periodStart, periodEnd).
+Fixed `every N` schedules anchor to the Unix epoch in UTC, shifted by `at HH:MM`.
+`every 7d` therefore fires on Thursday at 00:00 UTC (1970-01-01 was Thursday),
+not seven days after deploy. `every 7d at 08:00` stays Thursday 08:00 UTC.
+For another weekday, schedule daily and gate the job using day(periodEnd) % 7
+(Unix Thursday is 0, Monday is 4); never assume a deployment-time anchor.
+Calendar month/year schedules use UTC month boundaries; `guide time` has their syntax.
+
 ## Operations
 
 In tests, use `snapback4 clock advance <dur>` or `POST /dev/clock/advance`; each example line advances the running dev once.

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/guide/wave10_dx.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/guide/wave10_dx.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..2178b6f7d0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/guide/wave10_dx.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,50 @@
+use super::*;
+
+#[test]
+fn upgrade_card_version_and_nullable_count_are_bound_to_the_emitted_surface() {
+    let changes = card("changes").unwrap();
+    assert!(changes.starts_with(&format!("# Changes in {}\n", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"))));
+    assert_eq!(card("upgrade"), Some(changes));
+    for name in ["changes", "client", "cheatsheet"] {
+        let text = card(name).unwrap();
+        assert!(text.contains("hidden key") && text.contains("null") && text.contains("authorized missing count group") && text.contains('0'), "{name}");
+    }
+    let source = "table orders:\n  owner: principal\n  cents: int\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nview qtySum: sum orders of .cents by .owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nview qtyCount: count orders by .owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery roomCount(owner: principal):\n  return qtyCount[owner]\n";
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
+    assert!(snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound).contains("roomCount: Query<{ owner: string }, number | null>"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    let owner = args(&[("owner", id("dev:alice"))]);
+    assert_eq!(engine.query("roomCount", Context::new("dev:alice", owner.clone())).unwrap().data, Value::Int(0));
+    assert_eq!(engine.query("roomCount", Context::new("dev:bob", owner)).unwrap().data, Value::Null);
+    let write = format!("{source}table totals:\n  total: int\n  public\n  insert <- allow\nmutation copy(owner: principal):\n  return insert totals {{ total: qtySum[owner] }}\n");
+    let rejected = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), write.clone())]);
+    assert!(rejected.diagnostics[0].rewrite.contains("qtySum[owner] ?? 0"));
+    let repaired = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), write.replace("total: qtySum[owner]", "total: qtySum[owner] ?? 0"))]);
+    assert!(repaired.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", repaired.diagnostics);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn docs_use_engine_file_types_period_boundaries_and_projection_identity() {
+    let assets = card("assets").unwrap();
+    for mime in snapback4_core::assets::FILE_TYPES {
+        // Office types share an explicit family sentence rather than three long MIME spellings.
+        if mime.starts_with("application/vnd.") { continue; }
+        assert!(assets.contains(mime), "missing {mime}");
+    }
+    assert!(assets.contains("POST /assets") && !assets.contains("DX owns"));
+    for (alias, target) in [("optional param", "types"), ("mutation default", "writes"), ("service seat", "deploy")] {
+        assert_eq!(guide::resolve(alias), Some(target));
+    }
+    let source = "table rows:\n  title: text <=80\n  public\n  online only\nquery titles():\n  return rows first 10 by .id { title }\nquery identified():\n  return rows first 10 by .id { id, title }\njob sweep(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row] { title: 'done' }\nschedule weekly every 7d at 08:00: sweep\n";
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    let ts = snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound);
+    assert!(ts.contains("titles: Query<{}, { title: string }[]>"));
+    assert!(ts.contains("identified: Query<{}, { id: string; title: string }[]>"));
+    let schedule = &bound.schema.schedules[0];
+    assert_eq!(schedule.boundary(8 * 86_400_000), 7 * 86_400_000 + 8 * 3_600_000);
+    let jobs = card("jobs").unwrap();
+    assert!(jobs.contains("periodEnd` is the scheduled firing boundary") && jobs.contains("Thursday 08:00 UTC"));
+    assert!(card("types").unwrap().contains("not `format email`"));
+}

codex
The main behavior changes are clear: compiler checks now save upgrade baselines, React mutations wait for terminal receipts by default, and the browser witness can observe a form submission. I’m checking failure paths and races, particularly whether a failed check preserves the baseline and whether the form proof identifies the clicked write.

+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, symlinkSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { spawnSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { once } from 'node:events';
+import { createInterface } from 'node:readline';
+import vm from 'node:vm';
+import { settleMutation } from '../dist/write-settlement.js';
+import { sentResult, matchRead, otherwiseAll } from '../dist/contract.js';
+const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../', import.meta.url));
+const bin = process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN ?? join(root, 'target/debug/snapback4');
+const invoke = (args, cwd, env = {}) => spawnSync(bin, args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_BIN: bin, PORT_LO: undefined, PORT_HI: undefined, ...env }, timeout: 120000 });
+const success = result => assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr);
+const project = (t, name = 'dx10-') => { const dir = mkdtempSync(join(root, 'target', name)); t.after(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true })); return dir; };
+
+for (const state of ['sent', 'failed']) test(`run settlement owns a per-intent observer: ${state}, early receipt, concurrent calls and no read subscribers`, async () => {
+  const listeners = new Set(), submitted = [];
+  const client = { onWrite: fn => { listeners.add(fn); return () => listeners.delete(fn); }, mutate: async (op, args, options) => {
+    submitted.push(options.id);
+    return { state: 'pending', id: options.id };
+  } };
+  const first = settleMutation(client, { name: 'add' }, {}), second = settleMutation(client, { name: 'add' }, {});
+  let a = false, b = false; first.then(() => a = true); second.then(() => b = true);
+  await Promise.resolve(); assert.equal(listeners.size, 2); assert.notEqual(submitted[0], submitted[1]);
+  const outcome = id => state === 'sent' ? { state, id, seq: 1, result: null } : { state, id, why: { code: 'NO', family: 'rule', message: 'No' } };
+  for (const listener of listeners) listener(outcome(submitted[1]));
+  assert.deepEqual(await second, outcome(submitted[1])); assert.equal(a, false); assert.equal(b, true); assert.equal(listeners.size, 1);
+  for (const listener of listeners) listener(outcome(submitted[0]));
+  assert.deepEqual(await first, outcome(submitted[0])); assert.equal(listeners.size, 0);
+  client.mutate = async (op, args, options) => { for (const listener of listeners) listener(outcome(options.id)); return { state: 'pending', id: options.id }; };
+  assert.equal((await settleMutation(client, {}, {})).state, state); assert.equal(listeners.size, 0);
+  client.mutate = async () => { throw Error('admission threw'); };
+  await assert.rejects(settleMutation(client, {}, {}), /admission threw/); assert.equal(listeners.size, 0);
+  client.mutate = async () => ({ state: 'pending', id: 'custody' });
+  assert.equal((await settleMutation(client, {}, {}, { waitSeconds: 0 })).state, 'pending'); assert.equal(listeners.size, 0);
+});
+
+test('sentResult and compact matchRead preserve null, replay and empty-list meanings', () => {
+  assert.equal(sentResult({ state: 'sent', id: 'a', seq: 1, result: null }), null);
+  assert.equal(sentResult({ state: 'sent', id: 'a', seq: 1, replayed: true }), undefined);
+  for (const read of [undefined, { state: 'pending', id: 'a' }, { state: 'failed', id: 'a', why: {} }]) assert.equal(sentResult(read), undefined);
+  const handlers = { ...otherwiseAll(() => 'Unavailable'), absent: () => 'No notes', present: rows => `${rows.length} notes` };
+  assert.equal(matchRead({ data: [], complete: true, existence: 'absent' }, handlers), 'No notes');
+  assert.equal(matchRead({ data: [], complete: false, existence: 'partial' }, handlers), 'Unavailable');
+  assert.equal(matchRead({ denied: { code: 'NO' } }, handlers), 'Unavailable');
+  assert.equal(matchRead({ loading: true }, handlers), 'Unavailable');
+  assert.equal(matchRead({ data: [1], complete: true, existence: 'present' }, handlers), '1 notes');
+});
+

@@ -0,0 +1,86 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, symlinkSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { spawnSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { once } from 'node:events';
+import { createInterface } from 'node:readline';
+import vm from 'node:vm';
+import { settleMutation } from '../dist/write-settlement.js';
+import { sentResult, matchRead, otherwiseAll } from '../dist/contract.js';
+const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../', import.meta.url));
+const bin = process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN ?? join(root, 'target/debug/snapback4');
+const invoke = (args, cwd, env = {}) => spawnSync(bin, args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_BIN: bin, PORT_LO: undefined, PORT_HI: undefined, ...env }, timeout: 120000 });
+const success = result => assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr);
+const project = (t, name = 'dx10-') => { const dir = mkdtempSync(join(root, 'target', name)); t.after(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true })); return dir; };
+
+for (const mode of ['env', 'flag']) test(`portable ${mode} range sets URLs, Vite ports, launchers and proofs`, t => {
+  const dir = project(t);
+  success(invoke(['init', '--web', ...(mode === 'flag' ? ['--port-range', '30100-30109'] : [])], dir, mode === 'env' ? { PORT_LO: '30100', PORT_HI: '30109' } : { PORT_LO: '1', PORT_HI: '2' }));
+  const client = readFileSync(join(dir, 'src/client.ts'), 'utf8'), config = readFileSync(join(dir, 'vite.config.mts'), 'utf8');
+  assert.match(client, /127\.0\.0\.1:30100/); assert.ok(!config.includes('5173') && !config.includes('4173')); assert.match(config, /30101/);
+  const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, 'package.json')));
+  assert.match(pkg.scripts.backend, /--port 30100/); assert.match(pkg.scripts.dev, /--port 30101/); assert.match(pkg.scripts.preview, /--port 30101/); assert.match(pkg.scripts['proof:web'], /--port-range 30100-30109/);
+  assert.match(readFileSync(join(dir, 'README.md'), 'utf8'), /dev --port 30100/);
+  assert.match(readFileSync(join(dir, 'public/favicon.svg'), 'utf8'), /<svg/);
+  assert.match(readFileSync(join(dir, 'index.html'), 'utf8'), /favicon\.svg/);
+});
+
+test('invalid ranges refuse before writing; explicit ports must lie inside a range', t => {
+  for (const args of [['--port-range', '9-1'], ['--port-range', '0-10'], ['--port-range', '30000-30000'], ['--port-range', '30000-30009', '--port', '4400'], ['--port-range', '30000-30009', '--web-port', '5173']]) {
+    const dir = project(t); const result = invoke(['init', '--web', ...args], dir);
+    assert.equal(result.status, 2); assert.match(result.stderr, /E_INPUT/);
+    assert.throws(() => readFileSync(join(dir, 'package.json')));
+  }
+  const dir = project(t); assert.equal(invoke(['init', '--web'], dir, { PORT_LO: '30000', PORT_HI: '' }).status, 2);
+});
+
+for (const identity of ['use identity password', 'use identity guests', 'use personas alice']) test(`Expo sync detection and smoke use schema sign-in: ${identity}`, t => {
+  const dir = project(t); mkdirSync(join(dir, 'snapback'));
+  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'snapback/schema.q'), `${identity}\ntable notes:\n  owner: principal\n  title: text <=80\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery mine():\n  return notes where .owner = viewer first 20 by .id\nmutation add(title: text <=80):\n  return insert notes { owner: viewer, title }\n`);
+  success(invoke(['init', '--expo', '--port-range', '30100-30109'], dir));
+  symlinkSync(join(root, 'node_modules'), join(dir, 'node_modules'));
+  success(invoke(['check'], dir));
+  const typed = spawnSync(join(root, 'node_modules/.bin/tsc'), ['--noEmit'], { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 120000 }); success(typed);
+  const client = readFileSync(join(dir, 'src/client.ts'), 'utf8'), readme = readFileSync(join(dir, 'README.md'), 'utf8');
+  assert.ok(!client.includes('webDevice') && !readme.includes('web uses the wasm'));
+  assert.match(readme, /principal write journal/); assert.match(client, /principal write journal/);
+  const screen = readFileSync(join(dir, 'App.tsx'), 'utf8'), smoke = readFileSync(join(dir, 'src/native-smoke.ts'), 'utf8');
+  if (identity.includes('password')) {
+    assert.match(screen, /useNativeSmokeGuest\(\)/); assert.match(smoke, /auth\.signup/); assert.ok(!smoke.includes('auth.guest'));
+    const source = smoke.replace(/^import .*;\n/gm, '').replace('export function', 'function').replace(': string', '');
+    for (const segment of [null, 'prime', 'offline', 'restart']) {
+      const calls = [], ref = { current: false };
+      const context = vm.createContext({ useAuth: () => ({ state: 'signed-out', signup: (...args) => calls.push(args) }), useEffect: fn => fn(), useRef: () => ref, smokeSegment: () => segment, smokeAccount: () => ({ email: 'proof@example.test', password: 'test-only-password' }) });
+      vm.runInContext(source, context); vm.runInContext('useNativeSmokeGuest(); useNativeSmokeGuest()', context);
+      assert.equal(calls.length, segment === 'prime' ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+  } else if (identity.includes('personas')) assert.match(screen, /smokeSegment\(\) \? personas\[0\]/);
+  else assert.match(smoke, /auth\.guest/);
+});
+
+test('adapting a guest starter to password changes the untouched smoke helper too', t => {
+  const dir = project(t); success(invoke(['init', '--expo'], dir));
+  const schema = join(dir, 'snapback/schema.q'); writeFileSync(schema, readFileSync(schema, 'utf8').replace('use identity ', 'use identity password '));
+  success(invoke(['init', '--adapt'], dir)); assert.match(readFileSync(join(dir, 'src/native-smoke.ts'), 'utf8'), /auth\.signup/);
+});
+
+test('the published stopChild accepts handles and validates unknown shapes; serve launcher closes a real release', async t => {
+  const guide = readFileSync(join(root, 'crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing.md'), 'utf8');
+  const snippet = guide.match(/async function stopChild\([\s\S]*?(?=\/\/ A refusal)/)[0];
+  const context = vm.createContext({ spawn, once, createInterface, resolveBinary: () => bin, process, setTimeout, clearTimeout });
+  vm.runInContext(snippet + '\nglobalThis.helpers = { stopChild, startDev, startServe };', context);
+  const { stopChild, startDev, startServe } = context.helpers;
+  let stopped = false; await stopChild({ url: 'http://example', stop: () => { stopped = true; } }); assert.equal(stopped, true);
+  for (const invalid of [null, {}, { exitCode: undefined }, { exitCode: {}, signalCode: null, once() {}, kill() {} }]) await assert.rejects(stopChild(invalid), /stopChild needs/);
+  const dir = project(t); success(invoke(['init'], dir));
+  success(invoke(['deploy', join(dir, 'deploy')], dir));
+  const server = await startServe(join(dir, 'deploy')); t.after(server.stop);
+  assert.equal((await fetch(server.url + '/schema')).status, 401); await stopChild(server);
+  await assert.rejects(fetch(server.url + '/schema'));
+  const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', 'setInterval(()=>{},1000)'], { stdio: 'ignore' }); t.after(() => child.kill('SIGKILL'));
+  await stopChild(child); assert.notEqual(child.signalCode, null); await stopChild(child);
+});
+
diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wave10-upgrade.test.mjs b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wave10-upgrade.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..49020131d4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wave10-upgrade.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, symlinkSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { spawnSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { once } from 'node:events';
+import { createInterface } from 'node:readline';
+import vm from 'node:vm';
+import { settleMutation } from '../dist/write-settlement.js';
+import { sentResult, matchRead, otherwiseAll } from '../dist/contract.js';
+const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../', import.meta.url));
+const bin = process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN ?? join(root, 'target/debug/snapback4');
+const invoke = (args, cwd, env = {}) => spawnSync(bin, args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_BIN: bin, PORT_LO: undefined, PORT_HI: undefined, ...env }, timeout: 120000 });
+const success = result => assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr);
+const project = (t, name = 'dx10-') => { const dir = mkdtempSync(join(root, 'target', name)); t.after(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true })); return dir; };
+
+const aggregates = `use personas alice, bob
+ table orders:
+  owner: principal
+  cents: int
+  immutable owner
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  insert <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+view qtySum: sum orders of .cents by .owner
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+view qtyCount: count orders by .owner
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query roomCount(owner: principal):
+  return qtyCount[owner]
+mutation add(cents: int):
+  return insert orders { owner: viewer, cents }
+`.replace(' table orders:', 'table orders:');
+
+test('first check after version change reports new notes, API nullability and refusals without erasing the baseline', t => {
+  const dir = project(t); mkdirSync(join(dir, 'snapback')); const schema = join(dir, 'snapback/schema.q'); writeFileSync(schema, aggregates);
+  success(invoke(['check'], dir));
+  const path = join(dir, '.snapback4/check-surface.json'), saved = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path));
+  const old = { ...saved, version: '0.4.24', notes: {}, api: saved.api.replaceAll('number | null', 'number') };
+  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(old));
+  const changed = invoke(['check'], dir); success(changed);
+  assert.match(changed.stdout, /upgrade delta: 0\.4\.24 → 0\.4\.25/);
+  assert.match(changed.stdout, /N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION \+1/);
+  assert.match(changed.stdout, /Ops\.roomCount\.result: number → number \| null/);
+  const again = invoke(['check'], dir); success(again); assert.ok(!again.stdout.includes('upgrade delta'));
+  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(old));
+  writeFileSync(schema, aggregates + `table totals:
+  total: int
+  public
+  insert <- allow
+mutation copy(owner: principal):
+  return insert totals { total: qtySum[owner] }
+`);
+  const apiBefore = readFileSync(join(dir, 'snapback/generated/api.ts'), 'utf8');
+  const refused = invoke(['check', '--json'], dir); assert.equal(refused.status, 1);
+  assert.match(refused.stderr, /schema also changed/); assert.match(refused.stderr, /new refusals: E_QUARRY_TYPE \+1/);
+  assert.match(JSON.parse(refused.stdout).problems[0].rewrite, /qtySum\[owner\] \?\? 0/);
+  assert.equal(readFileSync(path, 'utf8'), JSON.stringify(old)); assert.equal(readFileSync(join(dir, 'snapback/generated/api.ts'), 'utf8'), apiBefore);
+  writeFileSync(schema, readFileSync(schema, 'utf8').replace('total: qtySum[owner]', 'total: qtySum[owner] ?? 0'));
+  success(invoke(['check'], dir));
+});
+
+test('upgrade captures the old installed compiler, checks the new one and points Exact at its rebuild', t => {
+  const dir = project(t); mkdirSync(join(dir, 'snapback')); writeFileSync(join(dir, 'snapback/schema.q'), aggregates);
+  success(invoke(['check'], dir));
+  const oldApi = readFileSync(join(dir, 'snapback/generated/api.ts'), 'utf8').replaceAll('number | null', 'number');
+  const packageRoot = join(dir, 'node_modules/snapback4'); mkdirSync(join(packageRoot, 'bin'), { recursive: true }); mkdirSync(join(packageRoot, 'dist'));
+  writeFileSync(join(packageRoot, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'snapback4', version: '0.4.24', exports: { './bin-path': './dist/bin-path.js' } })); writeFileSync(join(packageRoot, 'dist/bin-path.js'), '');
+  writeFileSync(join(packageRoot, 'bin/snapback4.js'), `const fs=require('fs'); const p=require('../package.json'); if(p.version==='0.4.24'){fs.writeFileSync('snapback/generated/api.ts',${JSON.stringify(oldApi)});console.log(JSON.stringify({ok:true,notes:[]}));}else{const r=require('child_process').spawnSync(${JSON.stringify(bin)},process.argv.slice(2),{encoding:'utf8'});process.stdout.write(r.stdout);process.stderr.write(r.stderr);process.exit(r.status);}`);
+  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ packageManager: 'npm@10', dependencies: { snapback4: '0.4.24' } }));
+  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'exact.mjs'), '// Exact application marker; HS owns the rebuild implementation.');
+  mkdirSync(join(dir, 'tools'));
+  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'tools/npm'), `#!${process.execPath}\nconst fs=require('fs');if(process.argv[2]==='view'){console.log('"0.4.25"');}else{for(const file of ['package.json','node_modules/snapback4/package.json']){const p=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file));if(p.dependencies)p.dependencies.snapback4='0.4.25';else p.version='0.4.25';fs.writeFileSync(file,JSON.stringify(p));}}`, { mode: 0o755 });
+  const result = invoke(['upgrade'], dir, { PATH: `${join(dir, 'tools')}:${process.env.PATH}` }); success(result);
+  assert.match(result.stdout + result.stderr, /Ops\.roomCount\.result: number → number \| null/);
+  assert.match(result.stdout, /Exact device rebuild: bun exact\.mjs snapback --rebuild/);
+  assert.equal(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, '.snapback4/check-surface.json'))).version, '0.4.25');
+});
+
diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wave10-witness.test.mjs b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wave10-witness.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..968c5d8b7f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wave10-witness.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,47 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, symlinkSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { spawnSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { once } from 'node:events';
+import { createInterface } from 'node:readline';
+import vm from 'node:vm';
+import { settleMutation } from '../dist/write-settlement.js';
+import { sentResult, matchRead, otherwiseAll } from '../dist/contract.js';
+const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../', import.meta.url));
+const bin = process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN ?? join(root, 'target/debug/snapback4');
+const invoke = (args, cwd, env = {}) => spawnSync(bin, args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_BIN: bin, PORT_LO: undefined, PORT_HI: undefined, ...env }, timeout: 120000 });
+const success = result => assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr);
+const project = (t, name = 'dx10-') => { const dir = mkdtempSync(join(root, 'target', name)); t.after(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true })); return dir; };
+
+for (const persona of [undefined, 'alice']) test(`default web starter ${persona ?? 'guest'} proves a visible labelled form and ships a favicon`, { timeout: 120000 }, t => {
+  const dir = project(t); success(invoke(['init', '--web'], dir)); symlinkSync(join(root, 'node_modules'), join(dir, 'node_modules')); success(invoke(['check'], dir));
+  success(spawnSync(join(root, 'node_modules/.bin/vite'), ['build'], { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 120000 }));
+  const proof = invoke(['witness', 'web', 'dist', ...(persona ? ['--as', persona] : []), '--mutation', 'add', '--args', '{"title":"Visible proof"}', '--field', 'Task', '--value', 'Visible proof', '--button', 'Add'], dir);
+  success(proof); assert.equal(JSON.parse(proof.stdout).proof, 'visible-form'); assert.equal(JSON.parse(proof.stdout).state, 'sent');
+  const wrong = invoke(['witness', 'web', 'dist', '--mutation', 'add', '--args', '{"title":"Other value"}', '--field', 'Task', '--value', 'Visible proof', '--button', 'Add', '--timeout', '5'], dir);
+  assert.equal(wrong.status, 1); assert.match(wrong.stderr, /E_WITNESS_ARGUMENT/);
+  if (persona) { const sdk = invoke(['witness', 'web', 'dist', '--as', 'alice', '--mutation', 'add', '--args', '{"title":"SDK proof"}'], dir); success(sdk); }
+});
+
+test('visible-form arming ignores old sent state, isolates the clicked mutation and cleans repeated observers', async t => {
+  const { registerWebWitness } = await import('../dist/witness-web.js');
+  const old = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'window');
+  const window = { location: { search: '?witness=web' }, statusText: 'sent' };
+  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'window', { configurable: true, value: window });
+  t.after(() => { if (old) Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'window', old); else delete globalThis.window; });
+  const listeners = new Set(), mutation = { kind: 'mutation', name: 'add' };
+  const original = async (op, args) => { for (const listener of listeners) listener({ state: 'sent', id: 'clicked', seq: 1, result: args }); return { state: 'pending', id: 'clicked' }; };
+  const app = { ready: Promise.resolve({ ok: true }), client: { viewer: () => 'dev:alice', onWrite: listener => { listeners.add(listener); return () => listeners.delete(listener); }, mutate: original } };
+  registerWebWitness(app, { add: mutation });
+  window.__snapback4Witness.beginForm('add', { title: 'new' });
+  let settled = false; window.__snapback4Witness.formResult().then(() => settled = true);
+  await Promise.resolve(); assert.equal(settled, false, 'existing sent text is not a write');
+  const returned = await app.client.mutate(mutation, { title: 'new' }); assert.equal(returned.state, 'pending', 'instrumentation preserves UI custody');
+  const receipt = await window.__snapback4Witness.formResult(); assert.equal(receipt.state, 'sent'); assert.equal(receipt.mutation, 'add');
+  assert.equal(app.client.mutate, original); assert.equal(listeners.size, 0);
+  window.__snapback4Witness.beginForm('add'); window.__snapback4Witness.beginForm('add');
+  await app.client.mutate(mutation, {}); assert.equal((await window.__snapback4Witness.formResult()).state, 'sent'); assert.equal(app.client.mutate, original);
+});
diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wire-envelopes.test.mjs b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wire-envelopes.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..4f35660e44
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/wire-envelopes.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, symlinkSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { spawnSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { once } from 'node:events';
+import { createInterface } from 'node:readline';
+import vm from 'node:vm';
+import { settleMutation } from '../dist/write-settlement.js';
+import { sentResult, matchRead, otherwiseAll } from '../dist/contract.js';
+const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../', import.meta.url));
+const bin = process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN ?? join(root, 'target/debug/snapback4');
+const invoke = (args, cwd, env = {}) => spawnSync(bin, args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_BIN: bin, PORT_LO: undefined, PORT_HI: undefined, ...env }, timeout: 120000 });
+const success = result => assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr);
+const project = (t, name = 'dx10-') => { const dir = mkdtempSync(join(root, 'target', name)); t.after(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true })); return dir; };
+
+const envelopeSchema = `use personas alice
+table notes:
+  title: text <=80
+  public
+  insert <- allow
+query list():
+  return notes first 10 by .id { id, title }
+mutation add(title: text <=80):
+  return insert notes { title }
+mutation nothing():
+  return null
+mutation refuse():
+  require false else PROOF_REFUSED
+  return null
+`;
+async function dev(dir) {
+  const child = spawn(bin, ['dev', '--memory', '--port', '0', '--no-watch'], { cwd: dir, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
+  child.stderr.resume();
+  const [url] = await once(createInterface({ input: child.stdout }), 'line');
+  return { url, stop: async () => { if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return; const done = once(child, 'close'); child.kill('SIGTERM'); await done; } };
+}
+test('guide testing full HTTP bodies match real routes, including null returns, refusals and omitted ids', async t => {
+  const dir = project(t); mkdirSync(join(dir, 'snapback')); writeFileSync(join(dir, 'snapback/schema.q'), envelopeSchema);
+  const server = await dev(dir); t.after(server.stop);
+  const guide = readFileSync(join(root, 'crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing.md'), 'utf8');
+  const examples = JSON.parse(guide.match(/```json\n(\{\n  "\/m\/add"[\s\S]*?)\n```/)[1]);
+  let rowId, store;
+  for (const route of Object.keys(examples)) {
+    const body = await (await fetch(server.url + route, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-snapback-persona': 'alice' }, body: JSON.stringify({ args: route === '/m/add' ? { title: 'Proof' } : {} }) })).json();
+    assert.ok(!('id' in body), 'missing request id is omitted, never null');
+    if (route === '/m/add') { rowId = body.result.id; store = body.store_id; }
+    const text = JSON.stringify(body).replaceAll(rowId, 'row1').replaceAll(store, 'store1');
+    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(text), examples[route], route);
+  }
+  const explicit = await (await fetch(server.url + '/m/nothing', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-snapback-persona': 'alice' }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: 'saved-intent', args: {} }) })).json();
+  assert.equal(explicit.id, 'saved-intent'); assert.equal(explicit.result, null);
+});
+

373-// takes a union or an array of reads of different queries:

388-// @ref LLP 3000.013#6.7 — one name for the outcome union, so helpers never fall back to `unknown`.
389-export type Outcome<R = unknown> = Write<R> | Signal<R> | Act<R>;
390-/** What `isFailed` reads from a mutation or action hook: its latest outcome. */

   * receipt has none). */
  last: Write<R> | undefined;
  inFlight: boolean;
}
/** What `useMutation(op)` returns, named by the operation, for props:
 * `function Row({ remove }: { remove: UseMutation<typeof api.remove> })`. */
export type UseMutation<O> = O extends Ephemeral<infer A, infer R> ? MutationHook<A, Signal<R>>
  : O extends Mutation<infer A, infer R> ? WriteHook<A, R> : never;
function useTrackedMutation<A, R>(op: Ephemeral<A, R>): MutationHook<A, Signal<R>>;
function useTrackedMutation<A, R>(op: Mutation<A, R>, id?: string): WriteHook<A, R> & { restoring: boolean };
function useTrackedMutation<A, R>(op: Mutation<A, R> | Ephemeral<A, R>, id?: string): MutationHook<A, Write<R> | Signal<R>> | WriteHook<A, R>;
function useTrackedMutation<A, R>(op: Mutation<A, R> | Ephemeral<A, R>, id?: string): MutationHook<A, Write<R> | Signal<R>> & { restoring?: boolean } {
  const client = useClient();
  const [last, setLast] = useState<Write<R> | Signal<R> | undefined>(undefined);
  const [inFlight, setInFlight] = useState(false);
  const restoration = useMemo(() => ({ pending: !!id }), [client, op, id]);
  const [, refreshRestoration] = useState(0);
  const lifetime = useRef({ active: true, pending: 0, next: 0, latest: 0, ids: new Map<string, number>(), early: new Map<string, Exclude<Write, { state: "pending" }>>() });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!lifetime.current.active) {
      lifetime.current = { active: true, pending: 0, next: 0, latest: 0, ids: new Map(), early: new Map() };
      setLast(undefined);
      setInFlight(false);
    }
    const owner = lifetime.current;
    const restoredLookup = () => {
      if (owner.active && lifetime.current === owner && restoration.pending) {
        restoration.pending = false;
        refreshRestoration(n => n + 1);
      }
    };
    if (id) owner.ids.set(id, owner.latest);
    let restored = false;
    const unsubscribe = op.ephemeral ? () => {} : client.onWrite((write) => {
      const run = owner.ids.get(write.id);
      if (run == null) {
        // Settlement can win the same turn in which mutate returns pending.
        // Keep that terminal result until the returned id binds it to a run.
        if (owner.pending > 0) {
          owner.early.set(write.id, write);
          if (owner.early.size > 256) owner.early.delete(owner.early.keys().next().value!);
        }
        return;
      }
      owner.ids.delete(write.id);
      if (write.id === id) { restored = true; restoredLookup(); }
      if (owner.active && lifetime.current === owner && run === owner.latest) setLast(write as Write<R>);
    });
    if (id) void client.write(id).then(write => {
      if (!restored && owner.active && lifetime.current === owner && owner.latest === 0) {
        setLast(write as Write<R> | undefined);
      }
    }).catch(() => { /* No receipt is not a failed mutation. Keep the subscribed outcome. */ }).finally(restoredLookup);
    return () => { owner.active = false; unsubscribe(); };
  }, [client, op, id, restoration]);
  const run = useCallback(async (args: A, options?: MutateOptions): Promise<Write<R> | Signal<R>> => {
    const owner = lifetime.current;
    const serial = ++owner.next;
    owner.latest = serial;
    owner.pending++;
    if (owner.active) setInFlight(true);
    let outcome: Write<R> | Signal<R>;
    try { outcome = op.ephemeral ? await client.mutate(op, args) : await settleMutation(client, op, args, options); }
    catch (error) {
      // A foreign Client can throw without exposing its minted id. Do not
      // invent an operation identity or claim that this is safe to retry.
      outcome = { state: "failed", id: "", why: { code: "E_CLIENT", family: "client", message: `The client did not return a write outcome; whether it was admitted is unknown. ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` } };
    }
    let visible = outcome;
    if (!op.ephemeral && outcome.id) {
      owner.ids.set(outcome.id, serial);
      const early = owner.early.get(outcome.id);
      if (early) {
        owner.early.delete(outcome.id);
        owner.ids.delete(outcome.id);
        visible = early as Write<R>;
      } else if (outcome.state !== "pending") owner.ids.delete(outcome.id);
    }
    owner.pending--;
    if (owner.active && lifetime.current === owner) {
      if (serial === owner.latest) setLast(visible);
      setInFlight(owner.pending > 0);
    }
    return outcome;
  }, [client, op]);
  return { run, last, inFlight, restoring: restoration.pending };
}

/** A write's latest outcome and in-flight calls, including terminal settlement. */
export function useMutation<A, R>(op: Ephemeral<A, R>): MutationHook<A, Signal<R>>;
export function useMutation<A, R>(op: Mutation<A, R>): WriteHook<A, R>;
export function useMutation<A, R>(op: Mutation<A, R> | Ephemeral<A, R>) {
  return useTrackedMutation(op);
}

/** Track one write from submit to sent/failed. Pass its saved mutation id to
 * recover after remount/reopen without submitting it again. Unknown ids leave
 * last undefined. pending includes the interval before mutate returns an id.
 * `restoring` is true while a saved id's recorded outcome is being looked up;
 * `last === undefined` means unknown only once `restoring` is false. */
export function usePendingWrite<A, R>(op: Mutation<A, R>, id?: string) {
  const write = useTrackedMutation(op, id);
  return { ...write, pending: write.inFlight || write.last?.state === "pending" };
}

/** Admitted writes, oldest first, optionally restricted to one mutation.
 * Starts with [] while reading the first snapshot. */
// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.3 — render queued intent without inventing a predicted row.
export function usePendingWrites<A = unknown, R = unknown>(op?: Mutation<A, R>): readonly PendingWrite<A>[] {
  const client = useClient();
  const source = useMemo(() => pendingWritesSource(client, op?.name), [client, op?.name]);
  return useSyncExternalStore(source.subscribe, source.getSnapshot, source.getServerSnapshot) as readonly PendingWrite<A>[];
}

/** Compose rows with scoped intent cards, including success awaiting observation.
 * Pass explicit row/intent keys; a mutation id is never a row id. A predicted
 * row suppresses its card only while present. Intents carry no row authority,
 * ordinal, delivery receipt or asset handle. Rows retain their supplied order;
 * remaining intents follow in outbox order. See `snapback4 guide client`. */
export function usePendingItems<Row, M extends Mutation<any, any>>(options: PendingItemsOptions<Row, M>) {
  const client = useClient(), intents = usePendingIntents(client, write => options.writes.some(op => op.name === write.op) && options.scope(write as PendingWrite<MutationArgs<M>>));
  // Only confirmed matching rows end the online session's presentation. The
  // local device ignores these hints and owns retirement through observed writes.
  const confirmed = new Set(options.rows.filter(row => !(row as { pending?: boolean } | null)?.pending).map(options.rowKey));
  const observed = intents.filter(item => item.phase === "settling" && options.writes.some(op => op.name === item.write.op)
    && options.scope(item.write as PendingWrite<MutationArgs<M>>) && confirmed.has(options.intentKey(item.write as PendingWrite<MutationArgs<M>>))).map(item => item.write.id);
  const key = JSON.stringify(observed);
  useEffect(() => { observeIntents(client, JSON.parse(key)); }, [client, key]);
  return pendingItems(intents, options);
}

// `npm run native:smoke` drives the app unattended: sign in as a guest when it asks.
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "snapback4/react";
import { smokeSegment } from "snapback4-expo/witness";

export function useNativeSmokeGuest() {
  const auth = useAuth(), attempted = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (smokeSegment() !== "prime" || auth.state !== "signed-out" || attempted.current) return;
    attempted.current = true;
    void auth.guest();
  }, [auth]);
}
import { requireOptionalNativeModule } from "expo-modules-core";
import { createWitness, type Witness, type WitnessContainer } from "./witness-core";
export type { Witness } from "./witness-core";

import { witnessRuntime } from "./witness-runtime";

let instance: Witness | null | undefined;
/** Null on ordinary launches and outside an iOS simulator driven by `snapback4 witness ios`. */
export function witness(): Witness | null {
  if (instance === undefined) {
    const container = requireOptionalNativeModule<WitnessContainer>("Snapback4");
    instance = container?.witnessLaunch && JSON.parse(container.witnessLaunch).smoke === true ? null : createWitness(container, witnessRuntime());
  }
  return instance;
}

/** The starter may use normal guest sign-in during prime; never on ordinary launches. */
export function smokeSegment(): "prime" | "offline" | "restart" | null {
  const container = requireOptionalNativeModule<WitnessContainer>("Snapback4");
  if (!container?.witnessLaunch) return null;
  const config = JSON.parse(container.witnessLaunch);
  return config.smoke === true && ["prime", "offline", "restart"].includes(config.segment) ? config.segment : null;
}

/** Simulator smoke uses its owned loopback backend; ordinary launches keep the app URL. */
export function smokeOrigin(fallback: string): string {
  const container = requireOptionalNativeModule<WitnessContainer>("Snapback4");
  if (!container?.witnessLaunch) return fallback;
  const config = JSON.parse(container.witnessLaunch);
  return config.smoke === true && /^http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:[0-9]+$/.test(config.origin) ? config.origin : fallback;
}

/** Disposable account supplied only by the owned prime smoke launch. */
export function smokeAccount(): { email: string; password: string } | null {
  const container = requireOptionalNativeModule<WitnessContainer>("Snapback4");
  if (!container?.witnessLaunch) return null;
  const config = JSON.parse(container.witnessLaunch);
  return config.smoke === true && config.segment === "prime" && typeof config.smokeAccount?.email === "string" && typeof config.smokeAccount?.password === "string" ? config.smokeAccount : null;
}

     2	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — resolve once, pin core and adapters to that same release.
     3	import { readFileSync, existsSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
     4	import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
     5	import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
     6	import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
     7	
     8	function run() {
     9	  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
    10	  if (args.length === 1 && args[0] === '--help') { console.log('usage: snapback4 upgrade [version]\nUpgrade snapback4 and every installed snapback4-expo adapter with npm, bun or pnpm; keep exact pins. Default: latest. Rebuild native apps after changing the adapter.'); return; }
    11	  if (args.length > 1 || (args[0] && !/^(latest|\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)$/.test(args[0]))) throw Error('E_INPUT: upgrade takes an exact version or latest');
    12	  let root = process.cwd();
    13	  while (!existsSync(join(root, 'package.json'))) { const parent = dirname(root); if (parent === root) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: no project package.json'); root = parent; }
    14	  const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
    15	  const schema = existsSync(join(root, 'snapback/schema.q'));
    16	  const cli = () => {
    17	    const require = createRequire(join(root, 'package.json'));
    18	    return join(dirname(dirname(require.resolve('snapback4/bin-path'))), 'bin/snapback4.js');
    19	  };
    20	  const check = () => spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli(), 'check', '--json'], { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8', stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
    21	  if (schema) {
    22	    // A previous package may predate snapshots. Capture its own compiler output
    23	    // before install; never infer old facts using the newly installed engine.
    24	    const old = check();
    25	    let facts;
    26	    try { facts = JSON.parse(old.stdout); } catch { /* Unavailable baseline remains explicit. */ }
    27	    const api = join(root, 'snapback/generated/api.ts');
    28	    if (facts && existsSync(api) && old.status === 0) {
    29	      const counts = values => (values ?? []).reduce((out, value) => { out[value.code] = (out[value.code] ?? 0) + 1; return out; }, {});
    30	      const require = createRequire(join(root, 'package.json'));
    31	      const version = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dirname(dirname(require.resolve('snapback4/bin-path'))), 'package.json'), 'utf8')).version;
    32	      const sources = readdirSync(join(root, 'snapback')).filter(file => file.endsWith('.q')).sort((a, b) => a === 'schema.q' ? -1 : b === 'schema.q' ? 1 : a.localeCompare(b)).map(file => [file, readFileSync(join(root, 'snapback', file), 'utf8')]);
    33	      mkdirSync(join(root, '.snapback4'), { recursive: true });
    34	      writeFileSync(join(root, '.snapback4/check-surface.json'), JSON.stringify({ version, sources, api: readFileSync(api, 'utf8'), notes: counts(facts.notes), refusals: counts(facts.problems) }));
    35	    } else console.log('upgrade: schema comparison baseline unavailable; old check did not complete');
    36	  }
    37	  const declared = pkg.packageManager?.split('@')[0];
    38	  const locks = [['bun', ['bun.lock', 'bun.lockb']], ['pnpm', ['pnpm-lock.yaml']], ['npm', ['package-lock.json', 'npm-shrinkwrap.json']]].filter(([, files]) => files.some(file => existsSync(join(root, file)))).map(([pm]) => pm);
    39	  if (declared && !['npm', 'bun', 'pnpm'].includes(declared)) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: supported package managers are npm, bun and pnpm');
    40	  if (!declared && locks.length > 1) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: multiple package-manager lockfiles; set packageManager in package.json');
    41	  const pm = declared ?? locks[0] ?? 'npm';
    42	  const dependencies = ['dependencies', 'devDependencies', 'optionalDependencies'];
    43	  const targets = ['snapback4', 'snapback4-expo'].filter(name => name === 'snapback4' || dependencies.some(key => pkg[key]?.[name]) || existsSync(join(root, 'node_modules', name, 'package.json')));
    44	  const invoke = (command, capture = false) => {
    45	    const result = spawnSync(pm, command, { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8', stdio: capture ? ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] : 'inherit' });
    46	    if (result.error || result.status !== 0) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${pm} ${command[0]} failed; check registry/tool availability`);
    47	    return result.stdout;
    48	  };
    49	  let version = args[0] ?? 'latest';
    50	  if (version === 'latest') {
    51	    const text = invoke(pm === 'bun' ? ['info', 'snapback4@latest', 'version', '--json'] : ['view', 'snapback4@latest', 'version', '--json'], true).trim();
    52	    try { const data = JSON.parse(text); version = typeof data === 'string' ? data : data.version; }
    53	    catch { throw Error('E_UPGRADE: package manager returned no exact core version'); }
    54	  }
    55	  if (typeof version !== 'string' || !/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$/.test(version)) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: registry returned no exact core version');
    56	  // Check the adapter release before any install changes package.json.
    57	  for (const name of targets) invoke(pm === 'bun' ? ['info', `${name}@${version}`, 'version', '--json'] : ['view', `${name}@${version}`, 'version', '--json'], true);
    58	  // Preserve each dependency section. One resolved version, even across sections.
    59	  const groups = new Map();
    60	  for (const name of targets) {
    61	    const sections = dependencies.filter(key => pkg[key]?.[name]);
    62	    if (sections.length > 1) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${name} occurs in multiple dependency sections; consolidate it first`);
    63	    const section = sections[0] ?? 'dependencies';
    64	    if (!groups.has(section)) groups.set(section, []);
    65	    groups.get(section).push(`${name}@${version}`);
    66	  }
    67	  for (const [section, packages] of groups) {
    68	    const mode = section === 'devDependencies' ? (pm === 'bun' ? '--dev' : '--save-dev') : section === 'optionalDependencies' ? (pm === 'bun' ? '--optional' : '--save-optional') : null;
    69	    invoke([pm === 'npm' ? 'install' : 'add', pm === 'bun' ? '--exact' : '--save-exact', ...(mode ? [mode] : []), ...packages]);
    70	  }
    71	  const after = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
    72	  for (const name of targets) if (!dependencies.some(key => after[key]?.[name] === version)) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${name} was not pinned to ${version}`);
    73	  const exact = existsSync(join(root, 'exact.mjs')) || existsSync(join(root, 'exact.json')) || dependencies.some(key => Object.keys(pkg[key] ?? {}).some(name => /^exact2?(?:$|[-/])/.test(name)));
    74	  if (exact) console.log('Exact device rebuild: bun exact.mjs snapback --rebuild'); // HS owns this command and the pairing gate.
    75	  if (schema) {
    76	    const checked = check();
    77	    if (checked.stderr) process.stderr.write(checked.stderr);
    78	    if (checked.stdout) process.stdout.write(checked.stdout);
    79	    if (checked.error || checked.status !== 0) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: packages pinned to ${version}; post-upgrade check refused. Repair the reported schema/device issues and run check again.`);
    80	  }
    81	  console.log(`upgrade: ${targets.join(', ')} pinned to ${version} with ${pm}; commit the lockfile, run check and acceptance tests${targets.includes('snapback4-expo') ? ', and rebuild the native app' : ''}`);
    82	}
    83	try { run(); } catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; }

#!/usr/bin/env node
// `npx snapback4 <verb>`: the Rust binary, found in the platform package
// installed beside this one, or named by SNAPBACK4_BIN, or built in this
// repository. The JavaScript here only finds and runs it, and the two live and
// die together: SIGINT, SIGTERM and SIGHUP to this wrapper are forwarded, and
// SIGKILL, which cannot be forwarded, still stops the binary: it is told this
// wrapper's PID (SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID) and exits when its parent is gone
// (Linux: the kernel's parent-death signal; elsewhere within 250 ms). So a
// recorded PID of either stops the server; `GET /dev/status` reports both
// (`pid` is the binary, `wrapperPid` this wrapper), and so does the stderr line
// `SNAPBACK4_READY … url=… pid=… wrapperPid=…`.
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { resolveBinary } from "../dist/resolve-binary.js";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { constants } from "node:os";

if (process.argv[2] === "web-shell") {
  try { await (await import("./web-shell.js")).runWebShell(process.argv.slice(3)); }
  catch (error) { console.error(`E_INPUT at web-shell: ${error.message}\n  rewrite: export a static app, then run snapback4 web-shell dist --base /`); process.exit(2); }
  process.exit(0);
}

let binary;
try { binary = resolveBinary(); }
catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exit(2); }
// Internal export helpers share the wrapper's kernel lease through fd 3.
const exportLease = process.argv[2] === "witness" && ["export-lease", "export-publish"].includes(process.argv[3]);
const child = spawn(binary, process.argv.slice(2), { stdio: exportLease ? ["inherit", "inherit", "inherit", 3] : "inherit", env: {
  ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_ACQUIRE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-acquire.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./upgrade.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_WEB_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-web.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid),
} });
const handlers = new Map(["SIGINT", "SIGTERM", "SIGHUP"].map(signal => [signal, () => { try { child.kill(signal); } catch { /* gone */ } }]));
for (const [signal, handler] of handlers) process.on(signal, handler);
// `npx snapback4 dev` puts npm and `sh -c` above this wrapper. A SIGTERM to the
// npx PID kills npm and the shell but never reaches us, so the server would keep
// serving as an orphan. Our parent's death is the stop signal. (`process.ppid` is
// read once at startup, so probe the original parent instead.)
const parent = process.ppid;
const parentAlive = () => { try { process.kill(parent, 0); return true; } catch (error) { return error.code === "EPERM"; } };
const orphanWatch = parent > 1 ? setInterval(() => {
  if (!parentAlive()) { try { child.kill("SIGTERM"); } catch { /* gone */ } }
}, 250) : null;
orphanWatch?.unref();
child.on("exit", (code, signal) => {
  if (orphanWatch) clearInterval(orphanWatch);
  for (const [name, handler] of handlers) process.off(name, handler);
  if (signal) {
    // Restore the default disposition before re-raising. Some platforms do not
    // terminate on this call; never let their empty event loop report success.
    const status = 128 + (constants.signals[signal] ?? { SIGHUP: 1, SIGINT: 2, SIGTERM: 15 }[signal] ?? 1);
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(status), 100);
    try { process.kill(process.pid, signal); } catch { process.exit(status); }
    return;
  }
  process.exit(code ?? 1);
});

    68	/// Wrap at `width`, never inside a `code span`.
    69	fn wrap(text: &str, width: usize) -> String {
    70	    let mut words: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    71	    for word in text.split(' ') {
    72	        match words.last_mut() {
    73	            Some(open) if open.matches('`').count() % 2 == 1 => { open.push(' '); open.push_str(word); }
    74	            _ => words.push(word.to_owned()),
    75	        }
    76	    }
    77	    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    78	    for word in &words {
    79	        match lines.last_mut() {
    80	            Some(line) if line.len() + 1 + word.len() <= width => { line.push(' '); line.push_str(word); }
    81	            _ => lines.push(word.clone()),
    82	        }
    83	    }
    84	    lines.join("\n")
    85	}

# Todos: a Snapback 4 Expo starter

An Expo (React Native) app over a local-first Snapback 4 backend: one table,
one live query and three mutations. Development and production builds open the
native device and work offline; Expo Go uses the online client; web uses the
wasm device.

> **`npm install` takes a few minutes and reports about 15 high-severity
> advisories. They are expected; do not run `npm audit fix --force`** (it
> "fixes" them by downgrading Expo to SDK 44). All come from two packages in
> Expo's development toolchain with no patched release; neither is in the app
> bundle. Details under [Audit findings](#audit-findings).

```sh
npm install
npx snapback4 check      # compiles snapback/schema.q, writes snapback/generated/api.ts
npx snapback4 dev        # serves http://127.0.0.1:4400 and reloads on save
npm start                # in a second terminal: Expo Go, or press w for web
npm run typecheck
```

`npm run ios:dev` builds the native development build. The iOS simulator
reaches http://127.0.0.1:4400 on this machine (`init --expo --port N` writes
that port; `EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` overrides it); for a physical phone set
`EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` to your TLS deployment. Choose "Continue as a
guest", then add a few todos.

## Files

- `snapback/schema.q`: the backend (tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations).
  Edit it and `snapback4 dev` re-adopts it; `snapback4 check` regenerates the API.
- `fixtures/`: seed rows and expected outcomes for `snapback4 test`.
- `src/client.ts`: one `createApp` for the whole app; it picks the native
  device, the online client (Expo Go) or `webDevice()` (web).
- `App.tsx`: sign-in, the live list (`usePage`) and writes (`useMutation`).
  `usePage` passes the query's cursor itself: a paged query takes `c: cursor ?`
  and reads `after c` (`npx snapback4 guide reads`); any other query reads
  with `useQuery`. Props name hook results by operation (`UsePage<typeof
  api.list>`, `UseQuery<…>`, `UseMutation<…>`), and `useSignedInViewer()` is
  the viewer below the sign-in. After you replace the schema,
  `npx snapback4 init --adapt` swaps this screen for a `Home` that compiles
  against any schema, while it is unmodified.
- `expo-env.d.ts`: Expo's types, including `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_*`.
- `metro.config.js` and `metro-wasm-transformer.cjs`: keep both; Metro then
  ships the web device's wasm and skips the local `.snapback4/` store.

Keep the Expo and `babel-preset-expo` major versions together; mixing them can
fail Hermes compilation.

## Audit findings

`package.json`'s `overrides` moves the config plugins' `xcode` from the
deprecated `uuid@7` to `uuid@11` (GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq). `npm audit` still
reports about 15 high findings from two advisories in Expo's development
toolchain, with no patched release of either package in any version:
`braces` (GHSA-vfj7-8cjw-p6xm, a stack-exhaustion denial of service on deeply
nested glob patterns; Metro's file watcher through `micromatch`) and
`node-forge` (GHSA-86w9-cpqp-85rv, RSA signature verification; the Expo CLI's
code-signing for updates). Neither is part of the app bundle, and the starter
passes neither untrusted patterns nor signatures to them. Do not run
`npm audit fix --force`: it "fixes" them by downgrading Expo to SDK 44.
`npx expo install --fix` takes Expo 57 patch releases as they ship.

## Native proof

`npm run export:ios` publishes Hermes to `dist/` and returns at every milestone;
native proof then runs in the background at background QoS, so you can keep
building. The next export and `npx snapback4 doctor` print its result; for a
final report run `npx snapback4 witness status --wait`. Receipts and logs stay
in `artifacts/native-smoke/<timestamp>`. `npm run native:smoke` explicitly
waits for a full Release build and smoke. Smoke proves the store opens,
acquisition, cold offline reopen and reconnect; pending write replay needs an
app scenario (`npx snapback4 guide witness`).

## Next steps

- Grow the schema: `npx snapback4 guide language`, then `guide rules`, `guide sync`.
- Try operations from the terminal: `npx snapback4 run add '{"title":"milk"}' --as alice`.
- Test without a server: `npx snapback4 test --fixture fixtures --as alice` loads
  `fixtures/todos.jsonl` and runs the expectations in `fixtures/test.cases.jsonl`
  (`npx snapback4 guide testing`).
- Add email accounts: change `use identity` to `use identity password` and see `guide auth`.
- A complete multi-screen example: `npx snapback4 init <dir> --expo --template chat`.
